				subtract_from_variable = { var = ITA_majority value = 4 }
				add_war_support = 0.03
			}
			else = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = ITA_air_tt_the_arm_is_an_office_with_a_budget_line
				add_political_power = 50
				add_to_variable = { var = ITA_majority value = 3 }
				add_stability = 0.02
			}
			ITA_machine_reckon = yes
			ITA_piazza_bands = yes
			ITA_chamber_bands = yes
			hidden_effect = {
				add_ai_strategy = {
					type = build_building
					id = air_base
					value = 20
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
